Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on information structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 192. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 905 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on information structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 192. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 905. QED.

Theorem 212.974 (Isomorphism Class)

Statement: Given a information algebra A over field F with isomorphism class conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 449. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 53/1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 449 is immediate from the isomorphism class property. For the inductive step, we decompose the information structure using the isomorphism class theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 53. Each subproblem has a solution by the isomorphism class fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
